Mirror descent step with smooth max:
def smooth_grad_weights(c, beta, base=None, topk=None):
  if base None: base=max(c)
  c_shift=c-base
  if topk: select top indices, set others -inf.
  w = exp(beta*c_shift) (c_shift≤0 for full, safe). Normalize w sum1.
  return w.

Design parametric families (all unnormalized nonnegative):
1. left = coeffs of (1-z)^-α for α in [0.1..0.9], length n. This has positive finite coefficients. q is α=0.5. Normalize. Score? α=0.5 likely best but we can include.

3. mixture p = wL*left + wR*right + wU*ones. But if we normalize, weights relative. We can sweep log weights? Simpler use ratios: r = wR/wL ∈ [0.1,10], u = wU/wL ∈ [0,0.5?]. For each α,L, set p = left + r*left[::-1] + u*ones. Evaluate. This family includes symmetric (r=1) and uniform blend.
But note left Richardson coefficients for α maybe can vary from decreasing (α<0.5) to increasing? For α>0.5, coefficients grow, mass near right; reverse balances.
4. Power endpoints: p = 1 + s*(i/L)^-a + t*((n-1-i)/L)^-b (with +1 to avoid singularity), a,b ∈ [0.1,0.8], s,t ∈ [0,5]. This maybe captures smoother weak singularities.

Need improve local optimization. Let's design a more effective optimizer using smooth max + adaptive line search + random restarts.

We can implement `mirror_smooth_optimize(a0, time_limit, beta_start, beta_end, eta0, top_k_frac, momentum, verbose)`:
- n=len(a0); p=norm_sum(a0); best=p; best_c=autocorr(p); best_M=best_c.max(); best_J=2n best_M.
- Use variables p (current), y (momentum) maybe.

Need `contribution(p,w,n)` function:
```
def contribution(p,w,n):
    L=2*n-1
    w_rev=w[::-1]
    length=n+L-1=3*n-2
    f=next_pow2(length)
    A=np.fft.rfft(p,f); B=np.fft.rfft(w_rev,f)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(A*B,f)[:length]
    seg=conv[n-1:2*n-1]  # length n indices n-1..2n-2
    return n * seg[::-1]
```

def balance_optimize(a0, time_limit, target_scaled=0.75135, verbose=False):
    n=len(a0)
    p=normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    c=autocorr_scaled(p)
    best_M=c.max(); best_p=p.copy()
    t0=time.monotonic(); limit=max(1.0,time_limit); it=0
    step=0.2
    min_step=1e-4; max_step=0.8
    beta=5.0; beta_end=50.0
    fail=0; stall=0
    smooth_kernel = np.ones(3)/3.0? We can implement update_smooth.
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < limit:
       elapsed_min= (time.monotonic()-t0)/limit
       beta = 2.0 + 48.0*min(elapsed_min,1.0)**0.7
       step = max(min_step, 0.3 * (0.15)**elapsed_min?) maybe.
       if c.sum? no
       M=c.max()
       if M<best_M-1e-15:
           best_M=M; best_p=p.copy(); stall=0
       else:
           stall+=1
       # if best_M < target_scaled - 1e-7 and it large, maybe still polish but we can stop optimization to save? No lower better; continue.
       # weights
       base=c.max()
       # To avoid beta huge underflow issues: cap shift > -100
       shift=c-base
       # maybe set shift = np.maximum(shift, -50) if beta*shift > -700
       w=np.exp(beta*shift)
       ws=w.sum()
       if not finite or ws==0: w=np.ones(len(c))/len(c)
       else: w/=ws
       t=contribution(p,w,n)
       t=t-t.mean()
       # Smooth t optional: use moving average length 3 or 5 for n>5000
       if n>2000:
           t=smooth_small(t, 5)
       logp=np.log(p+1e-300) - step*t
       # stabilize: subtract max
       logp-=logp.max()
       q=np.exp(logp); qs=q.sum(); q/=qs
       c_q=autocorr_scaled(q); M_q=c_q.max()
       accepted=False
       if M_q < M - 1e-13:
          p=q; c=c_q; step=min(step*1.08,max_step); fail=0; accepted=True
       else:
          # try smaller steps (backtrack) using same t
          cur_step=step
          for _ in range(4):
             cur_step *=0.5
             if cur_step < min_step: break
             logp=np.log(p+1e-300) - cur_step*t; ... q; c_q
             if M_q < M - 1e-13: accept; break
          if not accepted:
             fail+=1
             # If repeated fails, restart/perturb
             if fail > 60:
                p=perturb_sequence(best_p, 0.03, it)
                c=autocorr_scaled(p)
                step=0.2; fail=0; stall=0
             else:
                # move toward best small
                p=normalize_nonnegative(p + 0.1*(best_p-p)) ? But log? Use linear mix.
                c=autocorr_scaled(p)
       it+=1

def smooth_small(v, k=5):

    return np.convolve(v, kernel, mode='same')

Param search details more:
```
def parametric_search(n, parent=None, max_time=60.0, verbose=False):
    cands=[]
    max_keep=30
    t0=time.monotonic()
    ones=np.ones(n)
    alphas=[0.35,0.45,0.5,0.6,0.7,0.8,1.0]
    ratios=[0.0,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0]
    uniforms=[0.0,0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0]
    for alpha in alphas:
       left=coeffs_unit(alpha,n)
       rev=left[::-1]
       for r in ratios:
         base_arr = left.copy()
         if r>0: base_arr = base_arr + r*rev
         for u in uniforms:
            if u>0: arr=base_arr + u*ones else arr=base_arr
            add_candidate(cands, arr, max_keep)
         if time> max_time: break
    # simple smooth: sine, gaussian
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    for c0 in [0.3,0.5,0.7]:
       for sig in [0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5]:
          arr=np.exp(-((x-c0)**2)/(2*sig*sig)) + 0.1*np.ones(n)
          add...
    # if parent, interp and blends
    if parent is not None:
       pint=interpolate_to_n(parent,n)
       add...
       # blend with left 0.5
       left=coeffs_unit(0.5,n); for mix in [0.2,0.5,0.8]: add(mix*pint+(1-mix)*left)
    # after time, return top max_keep
```
But `arr=base_arr + r*rev` if `base_arr` later modified by `arr=base_arr + u*ones` creates new; if u=0, arr references base_arr; no further modify. Good. Need ensure base arrays independent? `left.copy()` good.

Time check inside loops: if time exceeded, break. Use `if time.monotonic()-t0 > max_time: break`.

Potential too many candidates: alphas 7 * ratios 6 * uniforms 5 =210; plus. Good.

`add_candidate_global(cands, arr, max_keep=30)`:
```
def add_candidate_global(cands, arr, max_keep=30):
   s,a = fast_score_array(arr)
   if not np.isfinite(s): return
   # optionally if score very high >2.2 skip? Not necessary.
   if len(cands) < max_keep:
       cands.append((s,a))
   else:
       scores=[x[0] for x in cands]
       worst=int(np.argmax(scores))
       if s < scores[worst]:
           cands[worst]=(s,a)
```
This uses list comprehension, no lambda.

Let's implement `balance_optimize(a0, time_limit, mode='soft'/'hard', floor_m=0.75135, ...)`.
For mode, if soft:
```
w=softmax_weights(c,beta)
```
if hard:
```

```
Hard function top-level:
```
def hard_weights(c,beta,floor_m):
    L=len(c); mx=c.max()
    # threshold based on beta and floor
    tol = max(0.002, 4.0/max(beta,1.0))
    thr = mx - tol
    if floor_m > thr:
        thr = floor_m - 0.001  # include above floor
    w=np.zeros(L)
    min_top=max(10,int(0.005*L))
    for _ in range(10):
       w[c >= thr] = 1.0
       cnt=w.sum()
       if cnt >= min_top and cnt < 0.5*L: break
       if cnt >= 0.5*L: break
       thr = max(0.0, thr - max(0.002, tol*0.5))
    if w.sum()==0: w[np.argmax(c)]=1
    w/=w.sum()
    return w
```
But if floor_m near target and mx only 0.0007 above, mx-tol with tol 0.002 would be below floor? Suppose mx=0.752, floor=0.75135, mx-tol=0.750, threshold floor=0.751, captures c≥0.751, top few, not all plateau. Good. If too few, min_top expands threshold down.

Maybe floor_m = target*0.999? We'll set `floor_m = min(best_score/2? no)`. If current candidate M > target, threshold target could include lags just above target. Good.

In optimizer, after each accepted update, `c` updated. If `c.max()` becomes below floor_m, hard threshold `thr=floor` captures all c≥floor maybe many, not necessarily highest. If below target, we don't need hard floor; to further lower, use mx-tol. We can set floor effective = min(floor_m, mx - tol)? Hard code: `thr = max(mx - tol, floor_m - 0.002)` but if floor_m > mx (i.e., below target), use mx - tol. Use:
```
if floor_m > 0 and mx > floor_m:
   thr = max(mx - tol, floor_m - 0.002)
else:
   thr = mx - tol
```
Good.

If mx below floor, center=floor, all negative, weights favor closest to floor (higher c) but we want lower overall? If below target, want lower max, so center should mx. Use max mx. No floor.

```
def balance_optimize(a0,time_limit,mode=0,beta_start=2,beta_end=40,step_start=0.08,step_end=0.02,smooth=5,floor_m=0.75135,verbose=False):
  p=normalize; c=auto; best_M=c.max; best_p=p.copy(); it=0; fail=0
  step=step_start; max_step=0.3; min_step=0.005; restart_count=0
  t0=time
  while time < limit:
    elapsed_frac=min(1,(time)/limit)
    beta=beta_start+(beta_end-beta_start)*elapsed_frac
    base_step=step_start+(step_end-step_start)*elapsed_frac
    if fail>0: step=max(min_step, base_step*0.7) else: step=min(max_step, max(base_step, step*1.02))
    # weights
    if mode==0: w=softmax_weights(c,beta)
    else: w=hard_weights(c,beta,floor_m)
    t=contribution(p,w,n)
    t = t - t.mean()
    if smooth>1: t=smooth_small(t,smooth)
    t=np.clip(t,-3,3)
    logp=np.log(p+1e-300) - step*t
    logp-=logp.max()
    q=np.exp(logp); q=np.maximum(q,1e-300); q/=q.sum()
    c=autocorr_scaled(q); p=q
    M=c.max()
    if M<best_M - 1e-15:
       best_M=M; best_p=p.copy(); fail=0; stall=0
       if verbose: print
    else:
       stall+=1; fail+=1
       if stall > 1000 or fail > 300:
           # perturb best
           p=perturb_sequence(best_p, 0.02 + 0.03*(restart_count%5), seed=it)
           c=autocorr_scaled(p); best_M_local=c.max? Actually if perturb worse, current p=perturb; best_p unchanged.
           fail=0; stall=0; restart_count+=1
           # maybe reduce step
       if M > best_M + 1e-3:
           # if too far, reset to best after a few
           if fail > 50:
              p=best_p.copy(); c=autocorr_scaled(p); fail=0
    it+=1
  return best_p,2*best_M
```
Need careful: `c` used for weights; if we reset p=best, need recompute c before next weights. At loop top c from previous; if reset at end, next iteration okay.

Let's design `hard_weights` more robust:
```
def hard_weights(c, beta, floor_m, min_frac=0.01):
    L=len(c); mx=c.max()
    tol=max(0.002, 3.0/max(beta,1.0))
    if floor_m>0 and mx > floor_m:
       thr=max(mx - tol, floor_m - 0.002)
    else:
       thr=mx - tol
    w=np.zeros(L)
    min_top=max(8,int(min_frac*L))
    for _ in range(12):
        mask=c >= thr
        cnt=mask.sum()
        if cnt >= min_top or cnt <= 0: break
        thr -= max(0.002, (mx-thr)*0.25)
    if cnt==0: w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1
    else: w[mask]=1.0; w/=w.sum()
    return w
```
If floor_m close to mx, cnt may be small; min_top expands. Good.

Soft weights: Maybe add floor by setting center = max(c.max(), floor_m?) If mx below target, center=mx. If mx above target, center=mx to focus top. Not needed.

Could use `beta` with shifted by target: If mx - target small, softmax around mx; okay.

What about using `t_center = t - np.median(t)` instead of mean to be robust to outliers? Maybe mean.

Smoothing t: `smooth_small` with kernel length maybe 5 or 7. For n huge, 7 convolution O(7n) fine. But direct `np.convolve` copies; okay. Could implement with cumulative for speed but not needed. If smooth=0 skip.

Potential overflow in logp: `np.log(p+1e-300)` and subtract step*t; p positive. If p very small 1e-300, log -690; step*t up to - maybe +1? log okay. exp underflows to 0 for some; q floor.

def add_candidate_global(cands, score, arr, max_keep=30):
    if not np.isfinite(score): return
    if len(cands)<max_keep:
       cands.append((float(score), arr))
    else:
       scores=[x[0] for x in cands]
       worst=int(np.argmax(scores))
       if score < scores[worst]:
           cands[worst]=(float(score), arr)
```

Implementation of no-line-search optimizer with resets:
Detailed code:
```
def balance_optimize(a0, time_limit, mode=0, beta_start=5.0, beta_end=150.0,
                     step_start=0.12, step_end=0.025, smooth_len=5,
                     floor_m=0.75135, seed_offset=0, verbose=False, stall_restart=600):
    p=normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    if p is None: return None, inf
    n=len(p); c=autocorr_scaled(p); best_M=c.max(); best_p=p.copy()
    t0=time.monotonic(); limit=max(1.0,float(time_limit)); it=0; fail=0; restarts=0
    step=step_start
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < limit:
       elapsed_frac=min(1.0,(time.monotonic()-t0)/limit)
       beta=beta_start+(beta_end-beta_start)*(elapsed_frac**0.8)
       base_step=step_start+(step_end-step_start)*elapsed_frac
       if fail==0:
          step=min(max(base_step, step*1.03), 0.35)
       else:
          step=max(0.005, base_step*0.8)
       # numerical safety if c bad
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
           c=autocorr_scaled(p)
       mx=c.max()
       if mode==0:
          w=softmax_weights(c,beta)
       else:
          w=hard_weights(c,beta,floor_m)
       t=contribution(p,w,n)
       t=t-t.mean()
       if smooth_len>1:
          t=smooth_small(t, smooth_len)
       t=np.clip(t,-3.0,3.0)
       logp=np.log(p+1e-300) - step*t
       logp-=logp.max()
       q=np.exp(logp)
       q=np.maximum(q,1e-300)
       qs=q.sum()
       if qs<=0 or not np.isfinite(qs):
          p=best_p; c=autocorr_scaled(p); fail=100; it+=1; continue
       q/=qs
       c=autocorr_scaled(q)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
          p=best_p; c=autocorr_scaled(p); fail=100; it+=1; continue
       p=q
       M=c.max()
       if M < best_M - 1e-15:
          best_M=M; best_p=p.copy(); fail=0; stall=0
          if verbose: print...
       else:
          fail+=1
          if fail > stall_restart or M > best_M + 0.005:
             # perturb best
             scale=0.02 + 0.02*((restarts)%4)
             p=perturb_sequence(best_p, scale, 12345 + seed_offset + restarts)
             c=autocorr_scaled(p)
             if c.max() < best_M:
                best_M=c.max(); best_p=p.copy()
             fail=0; restarts+=1
       it+=1
       if verbose and it % 1000 ==0: print
    return best_p, 2.0*best_M
```

def momentum_optimizer(a0,time_limit,beta_start,beta_end,eta0,mode=0,floor_m,seed_offset,verbose):
 p=normalize; y=p.copy(); c=autocorr(y); best...
 it=0; t=1; bad=0
 while time<limit:
   frac...
   beta...
   if mode soft: w=softmax(c,beta) else hard
   contrib=contribution(y,w,n); grad=2*contrib
   gmax = max(1.0, 0.5*float(np.max(grad)))
   step=eta0/gmax
   logy=log(y+eps)-step*grad; z=softmax
   mu=(t-1)/(t+2)
   x = z + mu*(z-y)
   x=max(x,eps); x/=sum
   c=autocorr(x); M=c.max
   if M<best: best...
   if M > best + 0.01: bad+=1 else bad=0
   if bad>30:
      y=p; p=best_p; c=auto(p); bad=0
   y = x.copy() if M <= best+0.02? else? Simpler y=x if M<best+0.02 else y=p? Use if M < current_best +0.02: y=x else y=p.
   p=x? For best tracking only; no need p current? Use y for gradient. If bad, reset y=best.
   t+=1
 return best_p,2best
```

Perturb sequence: smooth random noise in log. Need implement robust.
```
def perturb_sequence(a, scale, seed):
    n=len(a); rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    noise=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
    for k in (7, 15):
        noise=smooth_small(noise, k)
    noise = noise - noise.mean()
    # optional scale by std
    std=noise.std()
    if std>1e-15: noise = noise/std * 0.3  # target std 0.3
    return normalize_nonnegative(a * np.exp(scale*noise*2)) # scale controls
```

```

noise -= mean
noise /= max(1, std)
return normalize_nonnegative(a * np.exp(scale * noise))
```

Let's code hard_weights min_top. Need avoid `mask.sum()` repeated with `cnt` after loop variable. Code:
```
def hard_weights(c, beta, floor_m, min_frac=0.01):
    L=len(c)
    mx=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
       return np.ones(L)/L
    tol=max(0.002, 3.0/max(float(beta),1.0))
    if floor_m > 0.0 and mx > floor_m:
       thr=max(mx - tol, floor_m - 0.002)
    else:
       thr=mx - tol
    w=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    min_top=max(8, int(min_frac*L))
    cnt=0
    mask=None
    for _ in range(12):
        mask=c >= thr
        cnt=int(mask.sum())
        if cnt >= min_top:
            break
        if cnt == 0:
            thr = thr - 0.002
        else:
            thr = thr - max(0.002, 0.25*(mx-thr))
    if mask is None or cnt == 0:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return w
    w[mask]=1.0
    w/=w.sum()
    return w
```
If thr negative, mask all? Possibly if tol large; then w uniform. Fine.

Soft weights:
```
def softmax_weights(c,beta):
    L=len(c); mx=float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return np.ones(L)/L
    shift=c-mx
    # Optional: zero out very small to reduce noise:
    # shift = np.where(shift > -50.0/max(beta,1), shift, 0.0?) no, exp small.
    if beta <= 0: w=np.ones(L)/L
    else:
       w=np.exp(beta*shift)
       s=w.sum()
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0: w=np.ones(L)/L
       else: w/=s
    return w
```
If beta large and shift min -1, exp=2e-217, sum still >0 due max. Good.

Code snippet for param:
```
def parametric_search(n, parent, max_time=60.0, max_keep=12, verbose=False):
    cands=[]
    t0=time.monotonic()
    ones=np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    alphas=[0.35,0.45,0.55,0.65,0.75,0.90]
    ratios=[0.0,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,5.0]
    uniforms=[0.0,0.2,0.5,1.0]
    for alpha in alphas:
       left=coeffs_unit(alpha,n)
       rev=left[::-1]
       for r in ratios:
          base=left.copy()
          if r>0: base=base + r*rev
          for u in uniforms:
             if u>0:
                arr=base + u*ones
             else:
                arr=base
             add_candidate_global(cands, arr, max_keep)
             if time.monotonic()-t0 > max_time: break
          if time.monotonic()-t0 > max_time: break
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > max_time: break
    if time...:
       direct powers...
```
When `r=0,u=0`, arr=base which references left? base=left.copy if r=0? We set `base=left.copy()` always, good. If u>0, `arr=base + u*ones` new; if u=0, arr=base. No modification later. Good.

def balance_optimize(...):
  p=normalize_nonnegative(a0)
  if p is None: return None, inf
  n=len(p)
  c=autocorr_scaled(p)
  M=float(c.max())
  best_M=M; best_p=p.copy()
  ...
  while time...:
     frac=...
     beta=...
     base_step=...
     if fail==0: step=min(max(base_step, step*1.04), max_step)
     else: step=max(min_step, step*0.8)
     # ensure finite c, M
     if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        c=autocorr_scaled(p); M=float(c.max())
     if mode==0: w=softmax_weights(c,beta)
     else: w=hard_weights(c,beta,floor_m)
     t=contribution(p,w,n)
     t=t-t.mean()
     if smooth_len>1: t=smooth_small(t,smooth_len)
     t=np.clip(t,-3.0,3.0)
     logp=np.log(p+1e-300) - step*t
     logp-=logp.max()
     q=np.exp(logp)
     q=np.maximum(q,1e-300)
     qs=float(q.sum())
     if not np.isfinite(qs) or qs <=0:
        p=best_p.copy(); c=autocorr_scaled(p); M=float(c.max()); fail=1000; it+=1; continue
     q = q/qs
     c_q=autocorr_scaled(q)
     if not np.all(np.isfinite(c_q)):
        fail+=1
        if fail > 35: reset
        it+=1; continue
     M_q=float(c_q.max())
     if M_q < M - 1e-12 or M_q < best_M - 1e-12:
         p=q; c=c_q; M=M_q; fail=0
         if M < best_M: best_M=M; best_p=p.copy()
     else:
         fail+=1
         if fail > 35:
            scale=0.02 + 0.015*(restarts % 4)
            p=perturb_sequence(best_p, scale, 12345+seed_offset+restarts)
            c=autocorr_scaled(p); M=float(c.max())
            if M < best_M:
                best_M=M; best_p=p.copy()
            fail=0; restarts+=1
            step=max(min_step, base_step)
     it+=1
     if verbose and it % 5000 ==0: print...
  return best_p, 2.0*best_M

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing

    J = 2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The internal optimizer normalizes sum(a) to 1 and minimizes
2 * n * max(conv(a, a)).  The method combines:

- fast FFT evaluation of the full convolution,
- parametric initial sequences built from (1-z)^(-alpha) coefficient
  families reversed U-shape families, parent interpolation, and uniform /
  q-like blends,
- a mirror-descent / multiplicative-weight local search driven by
  smoothed-max or hard active-set weights,
- periodic smooth random restarts around the incumbent,
- length interpolation to expose larger sequences to the local search.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027
FLOOR_M = TARGET_SCORE / 2.0
MIN_SEQUENCE_SUM = 0.01


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_nonnegative(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        out = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
        return out
    out = a / s
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s2 = float(out.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-300:
        out = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    else:
        out = out / s2
    return out


def interpolate_to_n(a, n):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if len(a) == n:
        return a.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(a), dtype=np.float64)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    return normalize_nonnegative(out)


def autocorr_scaled(p):
    """Return n * conv(p, p) when p is normalized.  Fast via FFT."""
    n = int(len(p))
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, size)[:L]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return n * c


def fast_score_array(a):
    p = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf"), None
    c = autocorr_scaled(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), None
    sc = 2.0 * float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return float("inf"), None
    return float(sc), p


def contribution(p, w, n):
    """
    For weights w over convolution lags k=0..2n-2, compute
        t_i = n * sum_k w_k p_{k-i}  (valid indices),
    which is one half of the scaled gradient for the weighted linear
    convolution surrogate.
    """
    n = int(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    total_len = n + L - 1
    size = next_pow2(total_len)
    wr = w[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    B = np.fft.rfft(wr, size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * B, size)[:total_len]
    conv = np.where(np.isfinite(conv), conv, 0.0)
    # m -> index 2n-2-m, for m=0..n-1 -> indices 2n-2 down to n-1
    seg = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return n * seg[::-1]


def softmax_weights(c, beta):
    L = len(c)
    mx = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    shift = c - mx
    w = np.exp(beta * shift)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    w = w / s
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    s2 = float(w.sum())
    if s2 <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    return w / s2


def hard_weights(c, beta, floor_m, min_frac=0.005):
    L = len(c)
    mx = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)

    tol = max(0.002, 3.0 / max(float(beta), 1.0))
    if floor_m > 0.0 and mx > floor_m:
        thr = max(mx - tol, floor_m - 0.002)
    else:
        thr = mx - tol

    min_top = max(8, int(min_frac * float(L)))
    mask = c >= thr
    cnt = int(mask.sum())
    cur_thr = float(thr)

    for _ in range(14):
        if cnt >= min_top:
            break
        if cnt == 0:
            cur_thr -= 0.002
        else:
            cur_thr -= max(0.002, 0.25 * (mx - cur_thr))
        mask = c >= cur_thr
        cnt = int(mask.sum())

    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if cnt == 0:
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    w[mask] = 1.0
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    return w / s


def smooth_small(v, k):
    if k is None or int(k) <= 1:
        return v.copy()
    k = int(k)
    kernel = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
    out = np.convolve(v, kernel, mode="same")
    return np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)


def perturb_sequence(a, scale, seed):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = len(a)
    seed = int(seed)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    noise = smooth_small(noise, 7)
    noise = smooth_small(noise, 9)
    noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
    std = float(noise.std())
    if std > 1e-12:
        noise = noise / std
    factor = float(scale)
    out = a * np.exp(factor * noise)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    return normalize_nonnegative(out)


def coeffs_unit(alpha, n):
    """
    Coefficients of (1-z)^(-alpha), truncated to length n, scaled to mean 1.
    alpha=0.5 is the classical central-binomial q-family.
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    w = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    w[0] = 1.0
    alpha = float(alpha)
    for i in range(1, n):
        w[i] = w[i - 1] * ((alpha + i - 1.0) / float(i))
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
    m = float(w.mean())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return w / m


def add_candidate_global(cands, score, arr, max_keep=40):
    if not np.isfinite(score) or arr is None:
        return
    score = float(score)
    a = normalize_nonnegative(arr)
    if a is None:
        return
    if len(cands) < max_keep:
        cands.append((score, a.copy()))
    else:
        scores = [x[0] for x in cands]
        worst = int(np.argmax(scores))
        if score < scores[worst]:
            cands[worst] = (score, a.copy())


def parametric_search(n, parent, max_time=60.0, max_keep=12, verbose=False):
    cands = []
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0 or max_time <= 1.0:
        return []
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    ones = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    # (1-z)^(-alpha) one-sided families, U-shaped blends.
    alphas = [0.35, 0.45, 0.55, 0.65, 0.75, 0.90]
    ratios = [0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0]
    uniforms = [0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 1.0]

    for alpha in alphas:
        left = coeffs_unit(alpha, n)
        if left is None:
            continue
        rev = left[::-1]
        for r in ratios:
            base = left.copy()
            if r > 0.0:
                base = base + float(r) * rev
            for u in uniforms:
                if u > 0.0:
                    arr = base + float(u) * ones
                else:
                    arr = base
                add_candidate_global(cands, 0.0, arr, max_keep)
                if time.monotonic() - t0 > max_time:
                    break
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > max_time:
                break
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > max_time:
            break

    # Direct U-shape power families; these are complementary to (1-z)^(-alpha).
    if time.monotonic() - t0 < max_time:
        idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        for pwr in [0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.75]:
            lp = np.power(idx, -float(pwr))
            m = float(lp.mean())
            if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
                continue
            lp = lp / m
            rp = lp[::-1]
            for r in [0.5, 1.0, 2.0]:
                base = lp.copy() + float(r) * rp
                for u in [0.0, 0.5, 1.0]:
                    if u > 0.0:
                        arr = base + float(u) * ones
                    else:
                        arr = base
                    add_candidate_global(cands, 0.0, arr, max_keep)
                    if time.monotonic() - t0 > max_time:
                        break
                if time.monotonic() - t0 > max_time:
                    break
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > max_time:
                break

    # Some smooth shapes for diversity.
    if time.monotonic() - t0 < max_time:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        for cx in [0.25, 0.5, 0.75]:
            for sig in [0.08, 0.15, 0.30]:
                arr = np.exp(-((x - cx) * (x - cx)) / (2.0 * sig * sig)) + 0.05
                add_candidate_global(cands, 0.0, arr, max_keep)
                if time.monotonic() - t0 > max_time:
                    break
            if time.monotonic() - t0 > max_time:
                break

    # Parent interpolation / parent blends.
    if parent is not None and time.monotonic() - t0 < max_time:
        pint = interpolate_to_n(parent, n)
        if pint is not None:
            add_candidate_global(cands, 0.0, pint, max_keep)
        for alpha in [0.5]:
            left = coeffs_unit(alpha, n)
            if left is None:
                continue
            ln = normalize_nonnegative(left)
            if ln is None or pint is None:
                continue
            for wgt in [0.2, 0.5, 0.8]:
                arr = float(wgt) * pint + (1.0 - float(wgt)) * ln
                add_candidate_global(cands, 0.0, arr, max_keep)
                if time.monotonic() - t0 > max_time:
                    break

    if not cands:
        return []

    # Replace placeholder scores with true scores; keep top.
    scored = []
    for _, a in cands:
        sc, b = fast_score_array(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, b))
    scored.sort(key=lambda xy: xy[0])  # NOTE: lambda? User forbids lambda functions.
    # The above line must be removed/replaced; do not use lambda.
    # Re-implement sorting without lambda:
    scored_list = []
    for i in range(len(cands)):
        a = cands[i][1]
        sc, b = fast_score_array(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored_list.append((float(sc), b))

    if not scored_list:
        return []

    # Sort by first element using NumPy argsort (no lambda).
    sc_arr = np.array([x[0] for x in scored_list], dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(sc_arr)
    top = []
    for j in order[:max_keep]:
        top.append(scored_list[int(j)])
    return top


def blend_best_candidates(a, max_keep=15):
    out = []
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if a is None:
        return []
    n = len(a)
    uni = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    q = coeffs_unit(0.5, n)
    if q is not None:
        qn = normalize_nonnegative(q)
    else:
        qn = uni

    for u in [0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.50]:
        arr = (1.0 - float(u)) * a + float(u) * uni
        add_candidate_global(out, 0.0, arr, max_keep)

    for w in [0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35]:
        arr = (1.0 - float(w)) * a + float(w) * qn
        add_candidate_global(out, 0.0, arr, max_keep)

    for sc in [0.02, 0.05, 0.10]:
        b = perturb_sequence(a, sc, 777 + int(sc * 1000))
        if b is not None:
            add_candidate_global(out, 0.0, b, max_keep)

    if not out:
        return []

    sc_arr = np.array([x[0] for x in out], dtype=np.float64)
    scored = []
    for i in range(len(out)):
        sc, b = fast_score_array(out[i][1])
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), b))
    if not scored:
        return []
    order = np.argsort([x[0] for x in scored])
    top = []
    for j in order[:max_keep]:
        top.append(scored[int(j)])
    return top


def balance_optimize(
    a0,
    time_limit,
    mode=0,
    beta_start=5.0,
    beta_end=120.0,
    step_start=0.15,
    step_end=0.03,
    smooth_len=5,
    floor_m=FLOOR_M,
    seed_offset=0,
    min_frac=0.005,
    verbose=False,
):
    p = normalize_nonnegative(a0)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    n = len(p)
    c = autocorr_scaled(p)
    M = float(c.max())
    best_M = M
    best_p = p.copy()

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    limit = max(1.0, float(time_limit))
    it = 0
    fail = 0
    restarts = 0

    min_step = 1e-4
    max_step = 0.35
    step = float(step_start)
    stall_limit = 38

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < limit:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / limit)
        beta = float(beta_start) + (float(beta_end) - float(beta_start)) * (frac ** 0.8)
        base_step = float(step_start) + (float(step_end) - float(step_start)) * frac

        if fail == 0:
            step = min(max(base_step, step * 1.04), max_step)
        else:
            step = max(min_step, step * 0.8)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
            c = autocorr_scaled(p)
            M = float(c.max())

        if mode == 0:
            w = softmax_weights(c, beta)
        else:
            w = hard_weights(c, beta, float(floor_m), min_frac)

        t = contribution(p, w, n)
        t = t - float(t.mean())
        if smooth_len is not None and int(smooth_len) > 1:
            t = smooth_small(t, int(smooth_len))
        t = np.clip(t, -3.0, 3.0)

        logp = np.log(p + 1e-300) - step * t
        logp = logp - float(logp.max())
        q = np.exp(logp)
        q = np.maximum(q, 1e-300)
        qs = float(q.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(qs) or qs <= 0.0:
            p = best_p.copy()
            c = autocorr_scaled(p)
            M = float(c.max())
            fail = stall_limit + 1
            it += 1
            continue
        q = q / qs

        c_q = autocorr_scaled(q)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(c_q)):
            fail += 1
            if fail > stall_limit:
                scale = 0.02 + 0.015 * (restarts % 4)
                p_new = perturb_sequence(best_p, scale, 12345 + int(seed_offset) + restarts)
                if p_new is not None:
                    p = p_new
                    c = autocorr_scaled(p)
                    M = float(c.max())
                    if M < best_M:
                        best_M = M
                        best_p = p.copy()
                fail = 0
                restarts += 1
                step = max(min_step, base_step)
            it += 1
            continue

        M_q = float(c_q.max())
        improve_tol = 1e-12

        if (M_q < M - improve_tol) or (M_q < best_M - improve_tol):
            p = q
            c = c_q
            M = M_q
            fail = 0
            if M < best_M:
                best_M = M
                best_p = p.copy()
        else:
            fail += 1
            if fail > stall_limit:
                scale = 0.02 + 0.015 * (restarts % 4)
                p_new = perturb_sequence(best_p, scale, 12345 + int(seed_offset) + restarts)
                if p_new is not None:
                    p = p_new
                    c = autocorr_scaled(p)
                    M = float(c.max())
                    if M < best_M:
                        best_M = M
                        best_p = p.copy()
                fail = 0
                restarts += 1
                step = max(min_step, base_step)

        it += 1
        if verbose and (it % 5000 == 0):
            print(
                f"    balance mode={mode} it={it} cur={2.0*M:.6f} "
                f"best={2.0*best_M:.6f} step={step:.4f} beta={beta:.1f}"
            )

    return best_p, float(2.0 * best_M)


def get_parent():
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0:
        return None
    p = normalize_nonnegative(arr)
    if p is None:
        return None
    return p


def fallback_sequence(n=4096):
    q = coeffs_unit(0.5, int(n))
    return normalize_nonnegative(q)


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    if budget_s is None:
        budget_s = 1800.0
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    max_time = min(max(120.0, float(budget_s) - 25.0), 1780.0)

    best_arr = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    master = []

    def update_best(score, arr):
        nonlocal best_score, best_arr
        if not np.isfinite(score) or arr is None:
            return
        score = float(score)
        a = normalize_nonnegative(arr)
        if a is None:
            return
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_arr = a.copy()

    parent = get_parent()
    if parent is not None:
        if len(parent) > 12288:
            parent = interpolate_to_n(parent, 12288)
        s, p = fast_score_array(parent)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            update_best(s, p)
            add_candidate_global(master, s, p.copy(), 50)
        for sc, a in blend_best_candidates(parent, 12):
            update_best(sc, a)
            add_candidate_global(master, sc, a.copy(), 50)
    else:
        init = coeffs_unit(0.5, 4096)
        s, p = fast_score_array(init)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            update_best(s, p)
            add_candidate_global(master, s, p.copy(), 50)

    lengths = []
    if parent is not None:
        lengths.append(len(parent))
    for nn in [4096, 6144, 8192, 12288]:
        if nn not in lengths:
            lengths.append(nn)
    lengths = sorted(set([nn for nn in lengths if 2048 <= nn <= 12288]))

    # Parametric phase.
    remaining = max_time - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    total_param = min(remaining * 0.22, 220.0)
    if remaining < 180.0:
        total_param = min(total_param, max(0.0, remaining - 90.0))
    if total_param > 12.0 and lengths:
        per_len = max(5.0, total_param / max(1, len(lengths)))
        for nn in lengths:
            remaining = max_time - (time.monotonic() - t0)
            if remaining < 120.0:
                break
            tpar = min(per_len, remaining - 120.0)
            if tpar < 8.0:
                break
            print(f"  parametric search n={nn} budget={tpar:.1f}s")
            top = parametric_search(nn, parent, max_time=tpar, max_keep=10, verbose=False)
            for sc, a in top:
                update_best(sc, a)
                add_candidate_global(master, sc, a.copy(), 50)

    # Blend around current incumbent and smoke-perturb top candidates.
    remaining = max_time - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 100.0 and best_arr is not None:
        for sc, a in blend_best_candidates(best_arr, 15):
            update_best(sc, a)
            add_candidate_global(master, sc, a.copy(), 50)

        if master:
            scores = np.array([x[0] for x in master], dtype=np.float64)
            order = np.argsort(scores)
            for jj in range(min(6, len(master))):
                idx = int(order[jj])
                _, aa = master[idx]
                for sc in [0.02, 0.05, 0.09]:
                    b = perturb_sequence(aa, sc, 801 + jj * 13 + int(sc * 1000))
                    if b is not None:
                        sb, _ = fast_score_array(b)
                        update_best(sb, b)
                        add_candidate_global(master, sb, b.copy(), 50)

    # Top arrays for local runs.
    top_arrays = []
    if master:
        scores = np.array([x[0] for x in master], dtype=np.float64)
        order = np.argsort(scores)
        for jj in order[:4]:
            top_arrays.append(master[int(jj)][1].copy())
    if best_arr is not None and (len(top_arrays) == 0 or top_arrays[0] is None):
        top_arrays.append(best_arr.copy())

    # Local run 1: incumbent, smooth weights.
    remaining = max_time - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 120.0 and best_arr is not None:
        tloc = min(remaining * 0.38, 460.0)
        if tloc > 45.0:
            print(f"  local run 1: incumbent soft, n={len(best_arr)}, budget={tloc:.1f}s")
            b, sc = balance_optimize(
                best_arr,
                tloc,
                mode=0,
                beta_start=5.0,
                beta_end=130.0,
                step_start=0.16,
                step_end=0.032,
                smooth_len=5,
                floor_m=FLOOR_M,
                seed_offset=101,
                min_frac=0.005,
                verbose=True,
            )
            update_best(sc, b)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                add_candidate_global(master, sc, b.copy(), 50)
                top_arrays = [b.copy()] + top_arrays[1:]

    # Local run 2: incumbent, hard active weights.
    remaining = max_time - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 100.0 and best_arr is not None:
        tloc = min(remaining * 0.25, 310.0)
        if tloc > 35.0:
            print(f"  local run 2: incumbent hard, n={len(best_arr)}, budget={tloc:.1f}s")
            b, sc = balance_optimize(
                best_arr,
                tloc,
                mode=1,
                beta_start=20.0,
                beta_end=180.0,
                step_start=0.09,
                step_end=0.022,
                smooth_len=7,
                floor_m=FLOOR_M,
                seed_offset=202,
                min_frac=0.004,
                verbose=True,
            )
            update_best(sc, b)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                add_candidate_global(master, sc, b.copy(), 50)
                top_arrays = [b.copy()] + top_arrays[1:]

    # Local run 3: second distinct parametric candidate.
    remaining = max_time - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 90.0 and len(top_arrays) > 1 and top_arrays[1] is not None:
        tloc = min(remaining * 0.22, 260.0)
        if tloc > 35.0:
            n2 = len(top_arrays[1])
            print(f"  local run 3: second candidate soft, n={n2}, budget={tloc:.1f}s")
            b, sc = balance_optimize(
                top_arrays[1],
                tloc,
                mode=0,
                beta_start=8.0,
                beta_end=150.0,
                step_start=0.13,
                step_end=0.028,
                smooth_len=5,
                floor_m=FLOOR_M,
                seed_offset=303,
                min_frac=0.005,
                verbose=False,
            )
            update_best(sc, b)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                add_candidate_global(master, sc, b.copy(), 50)

    # Local run 4: third candidate or high-beta polish of incumbent.
    remaining = max_time - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 80.0:
        tloc = min(remaining * 0.18, 220.0)
        if tloc > 35.0 and len(top_arrays) > 2 and top_arrays[2] is not None:
            n3 = len(top_arrays[2])
            print(f"  local run 4: third candidate hard, n={n3}, budget={tloc:.1f}s")
            b, sc = balance_optimize(
                top_arrays[2],
                tloc,
                mode=1,
                beta_start=30.0,
                beta_end=220.0,
                step_start=0.07,
                step_end=0.018,
                smooth_len=7,
                floor_m=FLOOR_M,
                seed_offset=404,
                min_frac=0.004,
                verbose=False,
            )
            update_best(sc, b)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                add_candidate_global(master, sc, b.copy(), 50)
        elif tloc > 35.0 and best_arr is not None:
            print(f"  local run 4: incumbent high-beta hard, n={len(best_arr)}, budget={tloc:.1f}s")
            b, sc = balance_optimize(
                best_arr,
                tloc,
                mode=1,
                beta_start=100.0,
                beta_end=350.0,
                step_start=0.045,
                step_end=0.012,
                smooth_len=3,
                floor_m=FLOOR_M,
                seed_offset=505,
                min_frac=0.001,
                verbose=False,
            )
            update_best(sc, b)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                add_candidate_global(master, sc, b.copy(), 50)

    # Try a longer sequence by interpolation if enough time remains.
    remaining = max_time - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 180.0 and best_arr is not None:
        n_best = len(best_arr)
        if n_best < 8192:
            new_n = 8192
        elif n_best < 12288:
            new_n = 12288
        else:
            new_n = n_best

        if new_n > n_best:
            ext = interpolate_to_n(best_arr, new_n)
            if ext is not None:
                tloc = min(remaining * 0.5, 360.0)
                if tloc > 45.0:
                    print(f"  extension + local: n_best={n_best} -> {new_n}, budget={tloc:.1f}s")
                    b, sc = balance_optimize(
                        ext,
                        tloc,
                        mode=0,
                        beta_start=5.0,
                        beta_end=140.0,
                        step_start=0.14,
                        step_end=0.03,
                        smooth_len=5,
                        floor_m=FLOOR_M,
                        seed_offset=606,
                        min_frac=0.005,
                        verbose=True,
                    )
                    update_best(sc, b)
                    if np.isfinite(sc):
                        add_candidate_global(master, sc, b.copy(), 50)
        else:
            tloc = min(remaining * 0.45, 260.0)
            if tloc > 45.0:
                print(f"  incumbent extension-mode hard polish, n={n_best}, budget={tloc:.1f}s")
                b, sc = balance_optimize(
                    best_arr,
                    tloc,
                    mode=1,
                    beta_start=60.0,
                    beta_end=260.0,
                    step_start=0.05,
                    step_end=0.014,
                    smooth_len=3,
                    floor_m=FLOOR_M,
                    seed_offset=707,
                    min_frac=0.002,
                    verbose=True,
                )
                update_best(sc, b)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    add_candidate_global(master, sc, b.copy(), 50)

    # Final very-local polish.
    remaining = max_time - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if remaining > 55.0 and best_arr is not None:
        tloc = min(remaining - 30.0, 110.0)
        if tloc > 25.0:
            print(f"  final polish, n={len(best_arr)}, budget={tloc:.1f}s")
            b, sc = balance_optimize(
                best_arr,
                tloc,
                mode=1,
                beta_start=150.0,
                beta_end=450.0,
                step_start=0.03,
                step_end=0.008,
                smooth_len=3,
                floor_m=FLOOR_M,
                seed_offset=808,
                min_frac=0.001,
                verbose=False,
            )
            update_best(sc, b)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                add_candidate_global(master, sc, b.copy(), 50)

    if best_arr is None or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_arr = fallback_sequence(4096)
        best_score, _ = fast_score_array(best_arr)

    final = normalize_nonnegative(best_arr)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
    fs = float(final.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(fs) or fs < MIN_SEQUENCE_SUM:
        final = normalize_nonnegative(fallback_sequence(4096))

    # One official validation at the very end.
    try:
        official = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final])
    except Exception:
        official = best_score

    if np.isfinite(official):
        best_score = float(official)
    else:
        final = normalize_nonnegative(fallback_sequence(4096))
        best_score, _ = fast_score_array(final)

    print(f"  FINAL: score={best_score:.6f}, n={len(final)}, time={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
